- **etabli-decompose**: Part of Établi, invoked by etabli-orchestrator. Break approved requirements and their design spine into task files that parallel benches can execute independently, with dependencies, file conflicts and tiers made explicit, then validate the set deterministically, one milestone at a time by default, with a review note closing each. Use when work has been approved and needs splitting into tasks, when an existing task set needs checking before benches start, or when a merged milestone needs its review. Does not implement tasks or decide requirements. ([ancelin-code/etabli](https://github.com/ancelin-code/etabli/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/etabli-decompose) · 2 copies)
- **etabli-implement**: Part of Établi, invoked by etabli-orchestrator inside a bench. Turn one task into a validated change in the working tree: resolve its acceptance criteria, freeze the scope, implement, validate proportionally with the repository's own commands, check the diff back against the criteria, and hand off. Use when implementing a task file from a decomposed epic, or a single change with explicit acceptance criteria. Does not decide what to build, commit, push, or open a pull request. ([ancelin-code/etabli](https://github.com/ancelin-code/etabli/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/etabli-implement) · 2 copies)
- **etabli-orchestrator**: The project's delivery assistant and the entry point of Établi. Answers where the project stands, what to do next, and who is working on what, from specs/ and git; and routes any request to build or change the project through requirements, design, decomposition, benches and review, holding the approval gates and the bench policy. Use for "what's next", "what's the status", progress, blockers, or any new work on a project that has a specs/ directory or asks to start one. Delegates the work itself to the other Établi skills; it does not write requirements, code, or pull requests. ([ancelin-code/etabli](https://github.com/ancelin-code/etabli/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/etabli-orchestrator) · 2 copies)
- **etabli-refine**: Part of Établi, invoked by etabli-orchestrator inside a bench, after etabli-implement. Take the uncommitted change a task left, review it with fresh eyes against the task's acceptance criteria, fix what the review finds, simplify the change without altering its behaviour, re-run validation, and add what was done to the handoff report. Use after etabli-implement on the same task, in the same working tree. Does not re-implement the task, widen its scope, commit, push, or open a pull request. ([ancelin-code/etabli](https://github.com/ancelin-code/etabli/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/etabli-refine) · 2 copies)
- **etabli-spec**: Part of Établi, invoked by etabli-orchestrator. Turn an intent into a short requirements document, then record the design decisions and interface contracts that let parallel benches build compatible parts. Use when starting a project, when an ask is too vague to decompose, or when parallel work needs an agreed interface to build against. Does not break work into tasks or implement anything. ([ancelin-code/tiroir](https://github.com/ancelin-code/tiroir/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/etabli-spec) · 2 copies)
- **etabli-status**: Part of Établi, used by etabli-orchestrator and by developers directly. Claim an Établi task and read run state directly from git, with no shared status file and no model reasoning. Use when a developer picks up a task from a decomposed epic, when a launcher needs to know whether a task is free before starting a bench, or when asked who holds a task. Scripts only: it does not implement tasks or open pull requests. ([ancelin-code/etabli](https://github.com/ancelin-code/etabli/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/etabli-status) · 2 copies)
